#### New or materially sharper investigation questions {#fiveg-batch05-new-questions}

| ID and potential question | Why pursue it, and what could move it forward? | Forum starting points |
|---|---|---|
| **G117 — Which funding and interest record belongs to the particular work being assessed?** <span id="fiveg-g117"></span> | A headline describes an industry-sponsored journal; other posts concern review-panel memberships, commercial promotions and independent product reviews. Separate publisher, journal, author employment, study support, material provision and funder involvement in design or publication. Recover the dated disclosures and relevant records for each role, including advocacy and product interests. An absent disclosure remains an open record question. **Builds on:** [G017](#fiveg-g017) · [G033](#fiveg-g033) · [G081](#fiveg-g081) · [G089](#fiveg-g089). | [onawah #805](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page41#post1393185); [onawah #812](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page41#post1399397); [onawah #825](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page42#post1412129); [ExomatrixTV #853](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page43#post1445338); [ExomatrixTV #924](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page47#post1502707); [norman #942](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page48#post1524759); [ExomatrixTV #974](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page49#post1554362) |
| **G118 — Under what conditions can a terrestrial signal interfere with an aircraft radio altimeter?** <span id="fiveg-g118"></span> | Aviation reports span early warnings, rollout delays and later pilot concerns. Recover the actual terrestrial band, out-of-band emissions, receiver susceptibility, approach or flight scenario, tests, operational restrictions and mitigations. Follow effects on flight safety and service. A receiver-interference finding and a biological-exposure claim need their own measurements and causal paths. **Builds on:** [G001](#fiveg-g001) · [G060](#fiveg-g060) · [G070](#fiveg-g070) · [G095](#fiveg-g095) · [G108](#fiveg-g108). | [Hughe #802](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page41#post1391900); [onawah #889](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page45#post1476924); [JackMcThorn #893](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page45#post1478714); [onawah #894](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page45#post1479426); [ExomatrixTV #990](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page50#post1566250) |
| **G119 — What would make a crystal-formation demonstration informative about a biological question?** <span id="fiveg-g119"></span> | Posts present xylitol or water-crystallization demonstrations and infer consequences for living tissue. Recover the material, solution, exposure geometry, temperature, evaporation, vibration, imaging, sample selection and comparison. Use randomized, blinded repeat measurements and a specified structural endpoint; then identify the additional work needed to transfer a finding to the proposed tissue or function. **Builds on:** [G002](#fiveg-g002) · [G008](#fiveg-g008) · [G054](#fiveg-g054) · [G073](#fiveg-g073) · [G115](#fiveg-g115). | [James Newell #834](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page42#post1421662); [ExomatrixTV #835](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page42#post1421855); [ExomatrixTV #939](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page47#post1520443) |
| **G120 — Which explanations account for a secular change in measured body temperature?** <span id="fiveg-g120"></span> | A Firstenberg newsletter connects historical US and Tsimane temperature studies with electrification and wireless exposure. Recover the original cohorts, dates, instruments, measurement sites, age structure, infection burden, medicines and environmental conditions. Compare the original authors’ explanations with the later exposure hypothesis and specify a comparison that could distinguish them. **Builds on:** [G003](#fiveg-g003) · [G056](#fiveg-g056) · [G079](#fiveg-g079) · [G099](#fiveg-g099). | [onawah #847](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page43#post1438067) |
| **G121 — How are labour, extraction and manufacturing burdens distributed through the connectivity supply chain?** <span id="fiveg-g121"></span> | A documentary lead and a later consequence list identify mining, child labour, habitat loss, manufacturing pollution and waste. Trace the particular mineral, supplier, facility, product and affected community through recoverable records. Compare procurement choices, repair, reuse and practical alternatives, including the burdens of wired equipment and the accountability available to workers and residents. **Builds on:** [G023](#fiveg-g023) · [G026](#fiveg-g026) · [G031](#fiveg-g031) · [G105](#fiveg-g105). | [onawah #849](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page43#post1438354); [onawah #971](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page49#post1552313) |
| **G122 — Which exposure mode is implicated in an electric-vehicle health complaint?** <span id="fiveg-g122"></span> | A Nissan LEAF lawsuit report describes illness, disability and loss of work around a vehicle. Recover the complaint, vehicle configuration, traction system, inverter, wiring, low-frequency and RF measurements, operating conditions and clinical timeline. Distinguish the person’s reported experience, proposed mechanism and any court finding while following practical support and remedy. **Builds on:** [G001](#fiveg-g001) · [G010](#fiveg-g010) · [G011](#fiveg-g011) · [G064](#fiveg-g064) · [G066](#fiveg-g066) · [G097](#fiveg-g097). | [Hughe #851](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page43#post1441132) |
| **G123 — How could a proposed nonmonotonic exposure response be tested?** <span id="fiveg-g123"></span> | The no-dose-response essay cites inverse responses and windows in animal and cellular work. Recover each endpoint, waveform, power range, duration, background field, dosimetry and uncertainty. Specify the predicted response curve before replication, including null ranges and a result that would revise it. Compare what each cited experiment supports with the broader headline. **Builds on:** [G003](#fiveg-g003) · [G007](#fiveg-g007) · [G008](#fiveg-g008) · [G055](#fiveg-g055) · [G103](#fiveg-g103). | [onawah #872](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page44#post1459868); [onawah #876](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page44#post1461359) |
| **G124 — What need does a proposed industrial wireless link serve, and how do alternatives compare?** <span id="fiveg-g124"></span> | Lariliss identifies remote mining as a way to reduce dangerous work; hm337 asks what 5G adds to existing connectivity. Define the actual task, coverage, latency, reliability, worker exposure, fail-safe behaviour, access and cost. Compare the available wired and wireless designs with affected workers and communities, retaining both benefits and burdens as testable parts of the decision. **Builds on:** [G027](#fiveg-g027) · [G031](#fiveg-g031) · [G060](#fiveg-g060) · [G075](#fiveg-g075) · [G082](#fiveg-g082). | [onawah #845](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page43#post1429117); [Lariliss #871](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page44#post1459774); [hm337 #875](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page44#post1460773) |
| **G125 — What would establish the identity and origin of material observed in a vial or biological sample?** <span id="fiveg-g125"></span> | DaveToo asks why relatively few independent material analyses are available; later posts supply microscopy and spectroscopy claims. Recover sample provenance, chain of custody, preparation, storage, blanks, reference materials, instruments and competing identifications. Combine independent methods and replication. Follow both the cost and protection of researchers and how results can remain publicly accountable. **Builds on:** [G008](#fiveg-g008) · [G033](#fiveg-g033) · [G049](#fiveg-g049) · [G072](#fiveg-g072) · [G111](#fiveg-g111) · [G115](#fiveg-g115). | [ExomatrixTV #883](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page45#post1468322); [onawah #885](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page45#post1476220); [DaveToo #886](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page45#post1476232); [onawah #896](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page45#post1480227); [onawah #984](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page50#post1559453) |
| **G126 — Which physical steps would a proposed self-assembling circuit require?** <span id="fiveg-g126"></span> | DaveToo asks how a complex device could assemble in a body; Onawah explicitly says she does not know how before proposing why it might be concealed. Recover the claimed components, fabrication process, selectivity, energy, addressing, communication path, biological access and observable output. Preserve the unanswered mechanism question and assess any intent claim through its own records. **Builds on:** [G041](#fiveg-g041) · [G042](#fiveg-g042) · [G082](#fiveg-g082) · [G111](#fiveg-g111) · [G115](#fiveg-g115). | [hm337 #875](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page44#post1460773); [onawah #885](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page45#post1476220); [DaveToo #898](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page45#post1481006); [onawah #899](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page45#post1481016) |
| **G127 — What legal instrument would change a person’s rights or status after a technological intervention?** <span id="fiveg-g127"></span> | An interview predicts altered human status and corporate ownership; a later article invokes patents and an SEC filing. Recover the actual jurisdiction, enactment, judgment, contractual provision, defined object and enforcement route. Compare the cited text with the prediction, preserving the distinction between a device or invention, a company’s description and a person’s legal rights. **Builds on:** [G028](#fiveg-g028) · [G040](#fiveg-g040) · [G042](#fiveg-g042) · [G066](#fiveg-g066) · [G081](#fiveg-g081). | [onawah #900](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page45#post1481331); [onawah #940](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page47#post1522853); [Philippe #941](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page48#post1523283) |
| **G128 — How can an affected person participate when the consultation setting itself is inaccessible?** <span id="fiveg-g128"></span> | A schools webinar, disability advocacy and a consultant article raise barriers to meetings, education and public participation. Ask the people concerned which access they need. Recover notice, venue, remote or written routes, accommodation requests, responses and deadlines; follow whether they can contribute, receive a substantive answer and use an available remedy. **Builds on:** [G018](#fiveg-g018) · [G035](#fiveg-g035) · [G040](#fiveg-g040) · [G075](#fiveg-g075) · [G076](#fiveg-g076) · [G088](#fiveg-g088). | [onawah #826](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page42#post1415136); [onawah #891](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page45#post1477457); [onawah #928](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page47#post1506795); [ExomatrixTV #954](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page48#post1538133) |
| **G129 — How do a company’s investor disclosures compare with its consumer representations?** <span id="fiveg-g129"></span> | Posts contrast securities risk statements, marketing and insurance wording. Recover the dated filing, precise risk, product claim, policy type and intended audience. Distinguish a disclosed contingency, contractual allocation and measured harm; compare consistency, omissions, subsequent developments and available consumer remedies across the documents. **Builds on:** [G033](#fiveg-g033) · [G038](#fiveg-g038) · [G066](#fiveg-g066) · [G076](#fiveg-g076) · [G081](#fiveg-g081). | [ExomatrixTV #838](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page42#post1422884); [ExomatrixTV #910](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page46#post1493088); [ExomatrixTV #937](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page47#post1518803) |
| **G130 — Which stage of a citizens’ initiative can produce which institutional response?** <span id="fiveg-g130"></span> | European campaign calls give differing dates and participation descriptions. Recover the official registration, eligibility, collection window, thresholds, submitted statements, examination and response. Registration, validated support, a duty to respond and a resulting legal change are distinct stages. Preserve historical action calls as dated records and give participants an accurate route to the next available step. **Builds on:** [G035](#fiveg-g035) · [G036](#fiveg-g036) · [G040](#fiveg-g040) · [G067](#fiveg-g067) · [G080](#fiveg-g080) · [G094](#fiveg-g094). | [MartinNovotny #907](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page46#post1488320); [MartinNovotny #918](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page46#post1493928); [ExomatrixTV #933](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page47#post1510094) |
| **G131 — What could a temporary mast shutdown establish about a farm’s reported losses?** <span id="fiveg-g131"></span> | The French cattle-farm report describes illness, milk losses and a proposed two-month shutdown, followed by a challenge. Recover the actual orders, later disposition, operating log, herd denominator, veterinary findings, milk records, exposure and concurrent husbandry or infectious changes. A prospective comparison should define outcomes, comparable farms, stopping rules, welfare protection and the limits of a single-site result. **Builds on:** [G010](#fiveg-g010) · [G020](#fiveg-g020) · [G021](#fiveg-g021) · [G067](#fiveg-g067) · [G072](#fiveg-g072). | [Hermoor #952](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page48#post1533960) |
| **G132 — What explains a shielding product’s reported change in performance over time?** <span id="fiveg-g132"></span> | A newsletter reports Faraday-bag failures and a move from single to double layers; later posts debate shielding products and cages. Recover the specific fabric, seams, closure, wear, band coverage, test geometry, transmitter behaviour and instrument limits. Repeat measurements before attributing a change to network power, material degradation or another cause, and compare user costs and practical access. **Builds on:** [G017](#fiveg-g017) · [G054](#fiveg-g054) · [G055](#fiveg-g055) · [G068](#fiveg-g068). | [norman #878](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page44#post1466931); [Innocent Warrior #923](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page47#post1502681); [ExomatrixTV #924](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page47#post1502707); [Innocent Warrior #925](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page47#post1502839); [Nick Matkin #926](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page47#post1503455) |
| **G133 — How can an installation-linked case report support a useful follow-up?** <span id="fiveg-g133"></span> | John supplies a two-person microwave-syndrome case report; other posts describe symptoms, moving and accommodation needs. Preserve the clinical and exposure timeline, reported onset and recovery, other changes, measurement protocol and author interpretation. Follow functioning and support while designing a prospective comparison with predefined outcomes, suitable controls and participant consent. **Builds on:** [G011](#fiveg-g011) · [G013](#fiveg-g013) · [G016](#fiveg-g016) · [G018](#fiveg-g018) · [G072](#fiveg-g072) · [G085](#fiveg-g085). | [onawah #909](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page46#post1491904); [ExomatrixTV #954](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page48#post1538133); [ExomatrixTV #962](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page49#post1543075); [onawah #978](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page49#post1555712); [PlasmaVortex #986](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page50#post1562661) |
| **G134 — What does a matched-heating experiment resolve, and which exposure questions remain?** <span id="fiveg-g134"></span> | The reproduced cardiomyocyte paper compares microwave exposure, heating alone and controls, and includes a limitations section. Carry its actual absorbed power, temperature trajectory, field configuration, cell model and functional endpoints with the findings. Separate a mechanism experiment from a test of an everyday exposure; identify the power, waveform, time and replication work the authors leave open. **Builds on:** [G002](#fiveg-g002) · [G008](#fiveg-g008) · [G055](#fiveg-g055) · [G056](#fiveg-g056) · [G058](#fiveg-g058) · [G073](#fiveg-g073) · [G103](#fiveg-g103). | [ExomatrixTV #974](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page49#post1554362) |
| **G135 — Which device and energy path connect a phone command to a remotely triggered drug release?** <span id="fiveg-g135"></span> | The two Mike Adams reproductions cite an electro-responsive graphene-oxide drug-delivery paper and extend it to a mass-harm hypothesis. Recover the engineered carrier, payload, controller, power source, electrodes, applied voltage, coupling, cellular model and demonstrated result. Trace every additional step required for activation by an ambient network signal and assess proposed deployment through its own material and operational evidence. **Builds on:** [G027](#fiveg-g027) · [G041](#fiveg-g041) · [G042](#fiveg-g042) · [G082](#fiveg-g082) · [G111](#fiveg-g111) · [G116](#fiveg-g116). | [norman #996](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page50#post1577053); [ExomatrixTV #997](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page50#post1577136); [onawah #1000](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page50#post1578289) |
| **G136 — What would validate a blood-sample change as an exposure or treatment outcome?** <span id="fiveg-g136"></span> | A reproduced exploratory report distinguishes initial impressions from a later worsening result and reports no significant difference in one 5G comparison. Preserve those local null and revised observations. Recover draw and storage conditions, anticoagulation, temperature, timing, blinded scoring, reference samples, actual exposure, analytical identification and clinical meaning before using an image or clot description to guide an intervention. **Builds on:** [G008](#fiveg-g008) · [G011](#fiveg-g011) · [G050](#fiveg-g050) · [G054](#fiveg-g054) · [G056](#fiveg-g056) · [G072](#fiveg-g072) · [G079](#fiveg-g079) · [G115](#fiveg-g115). | [ExomatrixTV #831](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page42#post1418888); [ExomatrixTV #964](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page49#post1548698); [ExomatrixTV #980](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page49#post1556852); [onawah #984](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page50#post1559453) |

|---|---|---|

#### Three useful investigations to return to participants {#fiveg-batch05-pathways}

1. **An accessible decision packet.** Follow [G118](#fiveg-g118), [G128](#fiveg-g128) and [G130](#fiveg-g130) through an actual interference decision, consultation or citizens’ initiative. Return the governing text, the reasons, what participation can change, an accessible response route and the remaining unanswered question. Inspect the institution’s explanatory work alongside the participant’s submission.
2. **A small, reproducible research packet.** Combine [G117](#fiveg-g117), [G133](#fiveg-g133) and [G134](#fiveg-g134). Return the full study, funding and interests, methods, exposure, limitations, outcome and a feasible independent follow-up. Include a null or revised observation where the source supplies one. The people reporting symptoms retain a place in designing useful support and inquiry.
3. **A correction that reaches the next retelling.** Follow [G050](#fiveg-g050), [G081](#fiveg-g081), [G096](#fiveg-g096) and [G135](#fiveg-g135). Preserve the two-paper identity distinction, the actual environmental-cofactor hypothesis, John’s stated mission and product refusal, and the additional apparatus needed by a proposed remote effect. Then inspect the next comparable summary, including this editor’s, for retained corrections and qualifiers.

The bounded DEDDA result is specific: the two publication identities differ; the identity correction belongs in the account; no acknowledgement appears within the reviewed continuation; and a broader scientific or motive conclusion does not follow from that correction alone. Separate correspondence checks retained John’s refusal in #924, Nick’s reading limitation in #916, Onawah’s explicit connection beyond the interview in #944, and the owners of the reproduced experiences in #953 and #978. This is a corrigible comparison using conversational skill version 0.1.0, not a permanent judgment of a person or an answer to every underlying claim.

**Fifth-batch continuation:** page 51 was the next unexamined page after batch 05; it is now covered in the [final batch](#fiveg-avalon-batch-06). This completes the fifth ten-page batch. The growing map contains **136 potential investigation topics** from **1,000 indexed post containers**. Source relationships, access gaps and retained earlier corrections remain in the [scope index](https://github.com/deepethics/ACCM-Deep-Ethics-Project/blob/main/_data/fiveg_avalon_scope.json).

### Final batch: pages 51–56 — usable sources, institutional learning and a complete text-inventory route {#fiveg-avalon-batch-06}

**30 October 2026 · posts #1001–1114 · 8 new or materially sharper questions, G137–G144 · 49 source-linked extensions to earlier topics.**

The questions cover **[RF](https://www.icnirp.org/en/frequencies/radiofrequence/index.html)** (radiofrequency) exposure, research, public decisions and practical support.

This final range contains **114 post containers**. The retrieved pagination identifies page 56 as the last page; its final container is #1114. Displayed posting dates run from **23 September 2023 to 5 August 2026**, and a preceding post has an edit dated 7 August 2026. This pass screened public text in discussion order, including new material inside quotations and ordinary replies outside malformed message boxes. Long articles, the ordinance collection and bibliographies were skimmed for topic discovery. Recordings, image contents and most underlying case files remain further access layers. The selected primary-source checks below name the additional material actually examined.

A Google A.I. passage supplied by John on 30 October 2026 adds useful institutional research leads. They enter the work packets below through their original sources. John also requested removal of one accidentally repeated research-scope paragraph from his source record. One complete copy remains, and the prior committed version preserves the earlier state.

#### Correspondence that produces useful next work {#fiveg-batch06-process}

The [10+1]({{ '/NETWORK/ten-plus-one/' | relative_url }}) and [1888 intelligence-properties taxonomy]({{ '/1888/' | relative_url }}) remain active in the actual reading: notice an answer supplied later; distinguish a reported improvement from a controlled comparison; relate a standards criticism to the evidence and reply; imagine a feasible test; retain a correction; and examine what the next representation makes possible.

[Meta Processing]({{ '/NETWORK/meta-processing/' | relative_url }}) keeps the source, selection and resulting action inspectable. [Topologetics]({{ '/TOPOLOGETICS/' | relative_url }}) keeps the connecting source routes available. **[TCoAw]({{ '/NETWORK/tcoaw/' | relative_url }})** (Thinking, Consciousness, Awareness) keeps the selected focus temporary and the wider field available. [Healthy Ambiguity]({{ '/HEALTHY-AMBIGUITY/' | relative_url }}) gives room to understand, freedom to explore and responsibility for the next move.

**[PQCE]({{ '/FLAGGING-SYSTEM/' | relative_url }})** (Process Quality Calibration Evaluation) examines representation, evaluative authority, response to clarification and later conduct. John’s distinction between PQCE and flawed flagging governs this pass. [Beyond Agreemurmelism]({{ '/AGREEMURMELISM/Beyond-Agreemurmelism/' | relative_url }}) asks what an agreement or disagreement helps clarify or accomplish. [Symmetric Grace and Bidirectional Scrutiny]({{ '/NETWORK/asymmetric-grace/' | relative_url }}) keep dignity and correction available across participants, institutions and the evaluator, with responsibility proportionate to power and consequence.

The **[DEDDA]({{ '/DEDDA/' | relative_url }})** (Deep Ethical Drift Detector App) source-and-summary profile was used on this inventory’s representations. It retained John’s explicit anecdotal qualification in #1113, his video-description clarification in #1023, the later policy answer in #1081, and the actual working article destinations in #1110–1111. It also treats the requested duplicate removal as an authorized editorial change. These bounded comparisons remain corrigible alongside the inventory.

#### Contributions worth carrying forward {#fiveg-batch06-contributions}

- **A personal observation with its own limit intact.** In [#1113](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page56#post1694069), John describes choices made using a meter, wired internet and phone settings, and reports that his tinnitus disappeared. He explicitly calls the account anecdotal and non-conclusive, leaving its usefulness for others to decide. The experience supplies a timeline, practical choices and a possible follow-up without losing that qualification.
- **An answer that arrived later in the archive.** [#1081](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page55#post1635535) quotes Nick Matkin’s earlier question about accomplishments and supplies a worldwide-policy PDF. [#1082](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page55#post1635541) adds an extensive collection of local actions and ordinance routes. Carry these with the earlier mission and outcome discussion, then recover each original decision and what it changed in practice.
- **A historical source made navigable.** [#1033](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page52#post1598015) provides separate routes for the 1973 Warsaw symposium’s sessions, measurements, occupational/public-health material and conclusions. That gives the historical inquiry a tractable document packet with authors, countries and experimental conditions to recover.
- **Questions directed to an accountable recipient.** [#1046](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page53#post1601963) reproduces Jeff Chiacchieri and Susan Burke’s questions for Congress and the regulator. [#1032](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page52#post1598011) offers an offline PDF and a model document; [#1036](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page52#post1598021) gives testimony instructions for a dated bill. Their useful continuation is a delivery-and-response record and an accurate current participation route.
- **A concrete household decision.** [Ricker #1088](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page55#post1643687) has a utility notice and asks about opting out, outages, fire risk and radiation. Recovering the meter model, installation terms, electrical safety record, measurements and available choices would answer more of that request than another general argument about technology.
- **Keep the source owner with a shared description.** [#1023](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page52#post1585686) expressly clarifies that John quoted text beneath a video. The later two-part review in [#1110](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page56#post1681539)–[#1111](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page56#post1681540) names Fabien Deruelle and supplies its bibliography. Both are useful provenance contributions when material travels between platforms.
- **Carry support already offered.** [#1018](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page51#post1580352) expresses severe distress; [#1019](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page51#post1580468) responds with concern and encouragement. Later interview reporting in [#1091](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page55#post1649232) and [#1102](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page56#post1665501) raises disability, work and continuity-of-care questions. Follow the practical support and the causal investigation with the people concerned retaining agency.

| **G137 — Which technical path connects an emergency alert with a proposed physical effect?** <span id="fiveg-g137"></span> | The October 2023 discussion brings together a broadcast test, phone behaviour, biological mechanisms and dated predictions. Recover the alert specification, participating network generations, message transport, device response, emitted signal and exposure. | **G138 — What comparison could test a proposed contribution to violent behaviour?** <span id="fiveg-g138"></span> | Abondance asks whether an exposure could amplify predispositions; later material connects brain-development research with violence and school shootings. Define the proposed intermediate function and outcome, actual exposure and temporal sequence. Compare populations while accounting for age, density, deprivation, substances, sleep, media, reporting, access to weapons and other relevant conditions. A laboratory endpoint, an individual event and a population association each require their own connecting work. **Builds on:** [G008](#fiveg-g008) · [G012](#fiveg-g012) · [G014](#fiveg-g014) · [G056](#fiveg-g056) · [G069](#fiveg-g069) · [G072](#fiveg-g072) · [G099](#fiveg-g099). | [Abondance #1028](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page52#post1595773); [TargeT #1029](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page52#post1595826); [ExomatrixTV #1083](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page55#post1635549); [ExomatrixTV #1089](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page55#post1647122); [onawah #1090](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page55#post1647444); [ExomatrixTV #1110](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page56#post1681539); [ExomatrixTV #1111](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page56#post1681540) |
| **G139 — Who actually owns, operates and answers for a particular installation?** <span id="fiveg-g139"></span> | Interviews question whether named mast operators exist; local-policy collections identify applicant and property-owner responsibilities. Trace the observed equipment through site reference, company register, trading name, asset owner, licensee, contractor, permit and service contact. Return an intelligible responsibility chain and a route for obtaining a response, distinguishing an unresolved name from a demonstrated absence of an accountable entity. **Builds on:** [G026](#fiveg-g026) · [G035](#fiveg-g035) · [G040](#fiveg-g040) · [G044](#fiveg-g044) · [G066](#fiveg-g066) · [G109](#fiveg-g109). | [ExomatrixTV #1045](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page53#post1601634); [ExomatrixTV #1055](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page53#post1613778); [ExomatrixTV #1068](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page54#post1622742); [ExomatrixTV #1082](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page55#post1635541); [onawah #1093](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page55#post1650649) |
| **G140 — What connects a weather-modification reporting category to a demonstrated physical operation?** <span id="fiveg-g140"></span> | John supplies a reporting-law route, a government library and a military weather document. Recover the exact activity, energy source, coupling mechanism, atmospheric conditions, scale, experiment and result. Trace the separate steps from a reportable proposal through an attempted operation to a measured weather outcome, and identify the equipment required by any proposed telecommunications connection. **Builds on:** [G001](#fiveg-g001) · [G010](#fiveg-g010) · [G022](#fiveg-g022) · [G041](#fiveg-g041) · [G042](#fiveg-g042) · [G082](#fiveg-g082) · [G084](#fiveg-g084). | [ExomatrixTV #1051](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page53#post1608536) |
| **G141 — Which electrical failures and protections matter when a smart meter is installed or power returns?** <span id="fiveg-g141"></span> | Ricker receives an installation notice after repeated outages and asks about fire risk, product improvement and opting out. Recover the meter model, socket and wiring condition, installation procedure, surge and restoration behaviour, certification, incident findings and utility terms. Follow electrical and fire-safety questions alongside the separate RF measurements, cost, notice and available choice. **Builds on:** [G010](#fiveg-g010) · [G017](#fiveg-g017) · [G025](#fiveg-g025) · [G035](#fiveg-g035) · [G066](#fiveg-g066) · [G076](#fiveg-g076) · [G097](#fiveg-g097). | [ExomatrixTV #1078](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page54#post1632593); [ExomatrixTV #1079](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page54#post1632595); [Ricker #1088](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page55#post1643687); [ExomatrixTV #1098](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page55#post1656548); [ExomatrixTV #1099](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page55#post1656551) |
| **G142 — How independent are the evidential contributions behind a standards assessment?** <span id="fiveg-g142"></span> | The Google A.I. passage supplied by John points to a published co-authorship analysis. Reconstruct the selected corpus, authors, affiliations, committee memberships, citations, underlying datasets and replication relationships with dates. Test how conclusions change under alternative corpus and network definitions. Follow substantive inclusion and exclusion decisions as well as graph structure, so a reader can inspect how many distinct observations and judgments support a recommendation. **Builds on:** [G032](#fiveg-g032) · [G033](#fiveg-g033) · [G059](#fiveg-g059) · [G081](#fiveg-g081) · [G089](#fiveg-g089) · [G117](#fiveg-g117). | [Google A.I. research leads and original sources](#fiveg-batch06-google-leads) |
| **G143 — Which research questions remain supported after a major study or programme changes direction?** <span id="fiveg-g143"></span> | **[NTP](https://ntp.niehs.nih.gov/research/topics/cellphones)** (National Toxicology Program) findings and later research-funding claims raise a concrete agenda question. Recover original aims, completed outputs, announced follow-up, budgets, decisions, stated reasons and successor work. Identify which exposure, mechanism or population questions remain open, who can pursue them, and whether a published research promise receives a traceable completion, revision or explanation. **Builds on:** [G015](#fiveg-g015) · [G033](#fiveg-g033) · [G036](#fiveg-g036) · [G056](#fiveg-g056) · [G072](#fiveg-g072) · [G103](#fiveg-g103) · [G117](#fiveg-g117). | [ExomatrixTV #1037](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page52#post1598025); [ExomatrixTV #1089](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page55#post1647122); [ExomatrixTV #1105](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page56#post1667144) |
| **G144 — What happens to a specific methodological objection after an experienced expert raises it?** <span id="fiveg-g144"></span> | Lin’s standards critique, the later animal-review discussion and alternative scientific networks provide a temporal research route. Recover the exact objection, evidence then available, institutional reply, decision criterion, later study and next revision. Compare the reasoning at each date and what could change it; an expert’s former role helps locate the exchange while the substantive argument remains available for examination. **Builds on:** [G032](#fiveg-g032) · [G033](#fiveg-g033) · [G043](#fiveg-g043) · [G049](#fiveg-g049) · [G050](#fiveg-g050) · [G059](#fiveg-g059) · [G103](#fiveg-g103) · [G114](#fiveg-g114). | [ExomatrixTV #1105](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page56#post1667144); [ExomatrixTV #1111](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page56#post1681540) · [Google A.I. research leads and original sources](#fiveg-batch06-google-leads) |

|---|---|---|
| [G001](#fiveg-g001) — Which technology and frequency does each account concern? | TargeT asks which 5G band a claim concerns; John later explicitly notes fallback to other frequencies. Acoustic patents and ocean/sonar material add a different physical carrier. Preserve the particular radio band, acoustic wave, device and medium in each explanation. | [TargeT #1029](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page52#post1595826); [ExomatrixTV #1043](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page53#post1601058); [ExomatrixTV #1073](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page54#post1630234); [ExomatrixTV #1074](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page54#post1631803); [ExomatrixTV #1110](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page56#post1681539) |

#### Useful research leads from the supplied Google A.I. passage {#fiveg-batch06-google-leads}

The passage makes three investigations easier to identify: a temporal record of expert criticism, the independence of an evidence network, and the work an alternative scientific body can perform. Their value is in the source recovery and next comparison they enable.

| Work packet | Original starting points | A useful result to return |
|---|---|---|
| **A methodological criticism through time** | James C. Lin’s [2023 paper](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/36706903/) discusses RF standards, long-term protection and gaps at millimetre wavelengths. [Tom Butler’s submission](https://ec.europa.eu/health/scientific_committees/scheer/docs/emf2022/Butler_Submission.pdf) supplies historical references, including Lin’s earlier criticism. | Recover Lin’s original argument, the relevant guideline passage, response and subsequent revision. Compare like endpoints and exposure conditions. The working object is the objection and how it was handled. See [G144](#fiveg-g144). |
| **An inspectable evidence network** | Nordhagen and Flydal’s [co-authorship analysis](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/35751553/), DOI 10.1515/reveh-2022-0037, reports a 17-researcher core in the literature network they examined. | Reconstruct the corpus, dated affiliations, graph edges, committee roles and underlying datasets. Identify independent replications and the reasons particular findings entered or left the assessment. Publish a reproducible table and test alternative graph definitions. See [G142](#fiveg-g142). |
| **Alternative scientific capacity that produces a usable output** | **[ICBE-EMF](https://icbe-emf.org/who-we-are/)** (International Commission on the Biological Effects of Electromagnetic Fields) describes its founding in 2021, multidisciplinary remit and declaration of independence. Its stated work includes reviewing research, recommendations and scientific discussion. | Follow a specific recommendation through its protocol, evidence, disclosures, proposed protections, responses and corrections. Compare what becomes testable, accessible or practically useful. Connect [G032](#fiveg-g032), [G033](#fiveg-g033), [G117](#fiveg-g117) and [G144](#fiveg-g144). |

The [6 Intent Liability Mechanics]({{ '/NETWORK/intent-resonator/#six-intent-mechanics' | relative_url }}) offer a concrete record structure: instructions, incentives, mission/output discrepancies, risk-management priorities, reasoning assumptions and choices. For an assessment, collect the mandate and selection rules; support and interests; promised protection and observed coverage; priorities and omitted endpoints; assumptions and thresholds; and the decisions made after criticism. The [Forgiveness Protocol]({{ '/NETWORK/ten-plus-one/' | relative_url }}) preserves room to examine architecture, consequences and repair without requiring a prior determination of malice.

A productive comparison can show that a criticism was answered, that an answer missed its object, that a decision improved, or that the record remains unresolved. It should return a clearer question or usable source to the people affected.

It retains male-rat tumour findings, reports no detected behavioural or DNA-damage effects in a separate five-day follow-up with unsuccessful temperature measurement, and states that NTP/NIEHS have no additional RF exposure research planned. | Preserve the retrieved edition; compare the original studies, follow-up designs, data and research-agenda decisions. #### What the completed inventory makes possible {#fiveg-batch06-completion}

The six batches form one source-linked route through the public text of this thread: **56 pages, 1,114 indexed post containers and 144 potential investigation topics**, with **176 extensions** retaining later contributions to earlier questions. Topic counts describe the editorial map. Each entry keeps its own source, investigation value, proposed next step and relation to the earlier work.

Three useful returns are now possible: a historical source packet with original authors, countries and exposure conditions; a decision packet with authority, participation, reasons and outcomes; or a small research packet with methods, disclosures, findings and a feasible independent follow-up. The map also preserves practical help, refusals, requests, personal observations and corrections. Its value can be assessed through what becomes more understandable, recoverable or usable.

The DEDDA checks in this final range preserve John’s #1113 qualification and #1023 provenance clarification, and carry the later #1081 answer with its earlier question. Inspecting the actual #1110–1111 destinations produced a valid null: the article links already work with the complete identifier. No acknowledgement of the earlier #960 paper-identity correction was located in #1001–1114; later correction uptake remains unestablished. These are local source-and-summary comparisons using conversational skill version 0.1.0, not judgments of a person.

**End of the retrieved thread: [page 56](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page56), [post #1114](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?100537-Stop-5G-before-it-s-irreversible-/page56#post1723624).** This completes the requested final batch and the text inventory through that endpoint. If new posts are added, a later commissioned pass can resume after #1114 by rechecking page 56 first. The [scope index](https://github.com/deepethics/ACCM-Deep-Ethics-Project/blob/main/_data/fiveg_avalon_scope.json) retains source identities, dates, relations, access gaps, earlier corrections and the endpoint record.
